<script>

jQuery(function() {

  var latestTutsModule = jQuery("#HomeModules_latestTuts");
  var gridsWrapper = latestTutsModule.find('.gridsWrapper');
  latestTutsModule.on('click', '.loadTrigger', function() {

    var itemsCount = gridsWrapper.find('.item').length;
    var loaderBtn = jQuery(this);
    var limit = 5;
    var maxItems = 15;

    if (itemsCount > 15) return;

    // Disable Btn
    loaderBtn.attr("disabled", true);

    jQuery.ajax({
      url: ipb.vars['base_url'] + "app=forums&module=ajax&section=homeModules&do=latestTuts&skip=" + itemsCount,
      method: "POST",
      data: {
        secure_key: ipb.vars['secure_hash'],
      },
      beforeSend: function() {
        console.log(loaderBtn);
      },
      success: function(data) {
        if (data === 'nopermission') {
          alert("Oops, Something went wrong!");
          return false;
        }
        
        gridsWrapper.append(data);

        if (itemsCount < 15) {
           loaderBtn.attr("disabled", false);
        } else {
           loaderBtn.hide();
        }
      }
    });

  });
});
		</script>

<script>
function lazyYoutubeLoad () {

  var youtube = document.querySelectorAll(".lazyYoutubeWrapper");

  for (var i = 0; i < youtube.length; i++) {

    var wrapper = youtube[i];
  
    // Check if wrapper has already been loaded
    if (wrapper.classList.contains('loaded')) continue;
    
    var embedID   = wrapper.dataset.embed;
    var module    = wrapper.querySelector('.module');
    var sourceSD  = "https://img.youtube.com/vi/"+ embedID +"/sddefault.jpg";
    var sourceHQ  = "https://img.youtube.com/vi/"+ embedID +"/hqdefault.jpg";
    var image     = new Image();

    image.src = sourceSD;

    image.addEventListener( "load", function() {

      // Check if obtained image is smaller, use HQ source image
      if (image.width < 600) image.src = sourceHQ;

      // add image to module
      module.appendChild( image );

    }(i));

    module.addEventListener( "click", function() {

      var iframe = document.createElement( "iframe" );
      var embed = this.parentElement.parentElement.dataset.embed;

      iframe.setAttribute( "allow", "autoplay" );
      iframe.setAttribute( "frameborder", "0" );
      iframe.setAttribute( "allowfullscreen", "" );
      iframe.setAttribute( "webkitallowfullscreen", "" );
      iframe.setAttribute( "id", "ytplayer" );
      iframe.setAttribute( "class", "EmbeddedVideo" );
      iframe.setAttribute( "src", "https://www.youtube.com/embed/"+ embed +"?html5=1&rel=0&fs=1&showinfo=0&autoplay=1" );

      this.innerHTML = "";
      this.appendChild( iframe );
      
    }, false );

    // Mark wrapper as loaded
    wrapper.classList.add("loaded");

    console.log("loaded");
      
  };
}

(function() {
  lazyYoutubeLoad();
})();
</script>
